THIRD-PARTY SOFTWARE NOTICES: llama.cpp WEB UI (embedded in llama-server-impl.dll)
================================================================================

gametrans redistributes, unmodified, binaries from the official llama.cpp release b11624
(https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases/tag/b11624,
llama-b11624-bin-win-vulkan-x64.zip). The llama.cpp built-in web UI (tools/ui in the llama.cpp
source tree) is compiled into llama-server-impl.dll of that release. gametrans does not use the
web UI: it starts the server with --no-webui. The UI code is nevertheless present inside the
distributed DLL, so the notices of the third-party JavaScript packages bundled into it are
reproduced below. The llama.cpp licence itself is in LICENSE-llama.cpp.txt; notices for third-party native code are in
LICENSE-llama.cpp-third-party.txt.

The package list errs on the side of inclusion: some entries may not actually end up in the
bundle; listing a notice for absent code is harmless.

How to read this file: packages whose complete licence text is identical (after normalising line
endings and dropping leading/trailing blank lines) are grouped; each group lists its packages as
"name@version" and then gives the licence text once. Texts that differ in any way, including the
copyright line, are kept as separate groups. Where a package ships several root-level licence-related
files (e.g. LICENSE plus NOTICE or third-party notices), all of them are reproduced, each under a
"----- file name -----" heading. Licence files for assets that are not embedded in the DLL are omitted
(for pdfjs-dist: the CMaps, ICC profiles, standard fonts and .wasm binaries, which are fetched at run time);
the pdf.js wrappers whose JavaScript is embedded have their own groups. Notes in [square brackets] were added by the compiler of this
file and are not part of the upstream licence texts. Licence texts were taken from the published
npm packages (via unpkg.com) unless stated otherwise in the group.

Entries: 388   Distinct licence texts (groups): 238
Declared licences: MIT 307, ISC 44, Apache-2.0 13, BSD-3-Clause 11, OFL-1.1 1, BSD-2-Clause 5, LicenseRef-Adobe-Core14-AFM 1, Unlicense 2, (MPL-2.0 OR Apache-2.0) 1, 0BSD 1, MIT OR Apache-2.0 1, MIT OR NCSA 1
